============================================================
OPERATOR ANALYSIS
============================================================

--- Arithmetic ---
  add: Adds two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before summing.
  sqrt: Returns the non negative square root of x. Equivalent to power(x, 0.5); for signed roots use signed_
  log: Calculates the natural logarithm of the input value. Commonly used to transform data that has positi
  subtract: Subtracts inputs left to right: x ? y ? … Supports two or more inputs. Set filter=true to treat NaNs
  signed_power: x raised to the power of y such that final result preserves sign of x
  sign: Returns the sign of a number: +1 for positive, -1 for negative, and 0 for zero. If the input is NaN,
  reverse:  - x
  power: x ^ y
  multiply: Multiplies two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before multiplication
  min: Minimum value of all inputs. At least 2 inputs are required
  max: Maximum value of all inputs. At least 2 inputs are required
  inverse: 1 / x
  abs: Returns the absolute value of a number, removing any negative sign.
  densify: Converts a grouping field of many buckets into lesser number of only available buckets so as to make
  divide: x / y

--- Cross Sectional ---
  vector_neut: For given vectors x and y, it finds a new vector x* (output) such that x* is orthogonal to y
  zscore: Z-score is a numerical measurement that describes a value's relationship to the mean of a group of v
  winsorize: Winsorize limits values in a data to within a specified number of standard deviations from the mean,
  scale: Scales the input so that the sum of absolute values across all instruments equals a specified book s
  rank: Ranks the values of the input x among all instruments, returning numbers evenly spaced between 0.0 a
  quantile: Ranks and shifts a vector of Alpha values, then applies a chosen statistical distribution (gaussian,
  normalize: Centers a daily cross section by subtracting the market mean; optionally divide by the cross section

--- Group ---
  group_zscore: Calculates the Z-score of each value within its group, showing how far each value is from the group 
  group_backfill: Fills missing (NaN) values for instruments within the same group by calculating a winsorized mean of
  group_mean: Calculates the harmonic mean of a data field within each specified group.
  group_rank: Ranks each element within its group based on the input field, assigning a value between 0.0 and 1.0.
  group_scale: Normalizes values within each group to a range between 0 and 1, making data comparable across differ
  group_neutralize: Neutralizes Alpha values within each specified group by subtracting the group mean from each value. 

--- Logical ---
  if_else: The if_else operator returns one of two values based on a condition. If the condition is true, it re
  and: Returns 1 ('true') if both inputs are 1 ('true'). Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  equal: Returns 1 ('true') if input1 and input2 are the same. Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  or: Returns 1 if either input is true (either input1 or input2 has a value of 1), otherwise it returns 0
  not_equal: Returns 1 ('true') if input1 and input2 are different numbers. Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  not: Returns the logical negation of x. Returns 0 when x is 1 (‘true’) and 1 when x is 0 (‘false’).
  greater: Returns 1 ('true') if input1 is a larger than input2. Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  greater_equal: Returns 1 ('true') if input1 is a larger or the same as input2. Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  less_equal: Returns 1 ('true') if input1 is a smaller or the same as input2. Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  less: Returns 1 ('true') if input1 is a smaller than input2. Otherwise, returns 0 ('false').
  is_nan: If (input == NaN) return 1 else return 0

--- Time Series ---
  ts_zscore: Calculates the Z-score of a time series, showing how far today's value is from the recent average, m
  kth_element: Returns the K-th value from a time series by looking back over a specified number of (‘d’) days, wit
  ts_sum: Sum values of x for the past d days.
  ts_std_dev: Calculates the standard deviation of a data series x over the past d days, measuring how much the va
  ts_scale: Scales a time series to a 0–1 range based on its minimum and maximum values over a specified period,
  ts_rank: Ranks the value of a variable for each instrument over a specified number of past days, returning th
  ts_quantile: Calculates the ts_rank of the input and transforms it using the inverse cumulative distribution func
  ts_mean: Calculates the simple average (mean) value of a variable x over the past d days.
  ts_delta: Calculates the difference between a value and its delayed version over a specified period. Useful fo
  ts_step: Returns a counter of days, incrementing by one each day.
  ts_delay: Returns the value of a variable x from d days ago. Use this operator to access historical data point
  ts_backfill: Replaces missing (NaN) values in a time series with the most recent valid value from a specified loo
  ts_av_diff: Calculates the difference between a value and its mean over a specified period, ignoring NaN values 
  ts_arg_min: Returns the number of days since the minimum value occurred in a time series over the past d days. I
  ts_arg_max: Returns the number of days since the maximum value occurred in the last d days of a time series. If 
  ts_corr: Calculates the Pearson correlation between two variables, x and y, over the past d days, showing how
  days_from_last_change: Calculates the number of days since the last change in the value of a given variable.
  ts_count_nans: Counts the number of missing (NaN) values in a data series over a specified number of days.
  hump: Limits amount and magnitude of changes in input (thus reducing turnover)
  ts_covariance: Calculates the covariance between two time-series variables, y and x, over the past d days. Useful f
  ts_decay_linear: Applies a linear decay to time-series data over a set number of days, smoothing the data by averagin
  ts_product: Returns the product of the values of x over the past d days. Useful for calculating geometric means 
  ts_regression: Returns various parameters related to regression function
  last_diff_value: Returns the most recent value of x from the past d days that is different from the current value of 

--- Transformational ---
  trade_when: The trade_when operator changes Alpha values only when a specific condition is met, keeps previous v
  bucket: The bucket operator creates custom groups by dividing data into buckets (ranges) based on ranked val
